公司簡介及集團架構
Company Profile and Group Structure

華潤啤酒（控股）有限公司                              CHINA RESOURCES BEER (HOLDINGS)
                                          COMPANY LIMITED
華潤啤酒（控股）有限公司（「本公司」，連同                     China Resources Beer (Holdings) Company Limited (the
其附屬公司，統稱「本集團」）於香港聯合交                      “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”), listed
易所有限公司掛牌（股份代號︰00291），是                    on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (stock code:
華潤（集團）有限公司（「華潤集團」）屬下的                     00291), is a beer listed subsidiary company of China Resources
啤酒上市公司，專營生產、銷售及分銷啤酒                       (Holdings) Company Limited (“CRH”). The Group focuses on the
產品。華潤雪花啤酒有限公司（「華潤雪花                       manufacturing, sales and distribution of beer products. China
啤酒」）為本公司的全資附屬公司。                          Resources Snow Breweries Limited (“CRSB”) is a wholly-owned
                                          subsidiary of the Company.

本公司於二零一五年成功進行業務重組，                        In 2015, the Company successfully completed its business
轉型成為專注發展啤酒業務的企業，公司                        restructuring and transformed itself into a beer-focused enterprise,
名稱亦由「華潤創業有限公司」更改為「華潤                      and was renamed from “China Resources Enterprise, Limited” to
啤酒（控股）有限公司」。                              “China Resources Beer (Holdings) Company Limited”.

 本集團自一九九三年開始在中國發展啤                        The Group has been in the beer business in China since 1993. Its
 酒業務，旗下啤酒的總銷量自二零零六年                       total sales volume ranked No.1 in the China market since 2006.
 起連續多年位居中國市場第一。旗艦品牌                       The flagship brand “ 雪花 Snow” is the largest single beer brand by
「雪花 Snow」為全球銷量最高啤酒品牌。                     volume worldwide.

作為華潤集團的一份子，我們矢志與消費                        As a member of China Resources Group, we are dedicated to
者、股東、員工和商業夥伴一起引領商業進                       leading the business progress and building a better life together
步，共創美好生活，成為大眾信賴和喜愛的                       with our consumers, shareholders, staff and business partners, and
啤酒企業。                                     make the Group a trusted and beloved beer enterprise.




華潤（集團）有限公司                                   華潤啤酒（控股）                                   華潤雪花啤酒

                                                                                        Milestones in 2018



                                              依託於 2018 年品牌的優異表現，華潤雪花啤酒獲世界品牌實驗室
                                              頒發 2018 年度「中國品牌年度大獎－中國啤酒行業 NO.1」及「中國
                                              啤酒最具影響力品牌 TOP10」兩項大獎。
                                              Leveraging on the outstanding performance of its branding in 2018,
                                              CRSB won “China Brand Annual Award – China Beer Industry
                                              No.1” and “China Beer Most Influential Brand TOP 10” awards
                                              from the World Brand Laboratory of the year.

                                                                                                                        關於本集團


                                                                                                                   About the Group
 華潤雪花啤酒多家地區公司，包括河南、四川、哈爾濱分公司榮獲香港
 環保促進會頒發香港綠色企業大獎銅獎；杭州、深圳、西安分公司獲頒
 優異獎，以表揚其貫徹執行環保政策的優秀表現。
 The regional companies of CRSB in Henan, Sichuan and Harbin were
 granted the bronze medals, and the regional companies in Hangzhou,
 Shenzhen and Xi’an, were granted the merit awards at the Hong Kong
 Green Awards held by the Hong Kong Green Council. The companies
 were recognized for their outstanding performance in implementing
 environmental protection policy.

前景                                       PROSPECTS
二零一八年，中國經濟呈現「穩的格局、進                      In 2018, the Chinese economy remained steady while making
的態勢」，消費增長持續為經濟發展帶來動                      progress, and the consumption growth continued to drive
力。中國啤酒市場穩步從銷量高速增長階                       economic development. The Chinese beer market has steadily
段轉向高質量發展階段邁進，高檔啤酒市                       shifted from the stage of “high-speed volume growth” to “high-
場快速增長，個性化、多元化和高端化需求                      quality development”. The high-end beer market is growing
正在不斷提升，成本壓力也一直上漲。這些                      rapidly. The demand for personalized, diversified and premium
行業的轉變，所帶來的不僅是挑戰，也是千                      products is constantly increasing, and the cost pressure has also
載難逢的機遇。本集團相信，具前瞻性的管                      been rising. These transformation in the industry bring in not only
理和戰略舉措，能幫助本集團抓緊這些機                       challenges, but also valuable opportunities. The Group believes
遇，在未來行業中取得重要位置，並帶來可                      that management and strategic initiatives with forward-looking
觀的盈利增長。                                  vision can help the Group to seize these opportunities, consolidate
                                         its industry position and achieve remarkable profit growth in the

本集團於二零一七年啟動品牌重塑，豐富                       The Group staged a campaign to reposition its brand in 2017 to
產品組合，圍繞「未來由你定義」的主題推                      diversify its product portfolio with the launch of its new product line,
出「概念系列」新產品，並於二零一八年完                      “Concept Series”, with the theme “You Define Your Future”, and
成了「勇闖天涯 superX」和「匠心營造」的新                 the new products “Brave the World superX” and “Craftsmanship”
品上市。其中，   「勇闖天涯 superX」作為本集              in 2018. In particular, “Brave the World superX”, as the Group’s first
團品牌重塑的首支核心產品，精確瞄準年輕                      core product for brand repositioning, targets young consumers with
消費人群，發力互聯網營銷，通過「新潮發                      emphasis on internet marketing. Through the strategy of “trendy
佈會 + IP 化推廣 + 代言人粉絲經濟 + 場景營              product launch activity + IP-based promotion + spokesperson-fans
銷」，突破了傳統啤酒新品的營銷方法，以                      economy + scenario-based marketing”, it achieved a breakthrough
消費者的角度去創造符合習慣與興趣的營                       in the marketing of beer products, which created an innovative
銷方式，不僅實現了「勇闖天涯 superX」的                  marketing method that conformed to habits and interests from
成功上市，也為整個「雪花 Snow」品牌的價                   the perspective of consumers. Such move not only contributed
值感提升與煥新提供了強大動力。                          to the successful marketing of “Brave the World superX” but

風險管治架構                                   RISK GOVERNANCE STRUCTURE
本集團的風險管治架構建基於「三道防線」                      The Group’s risk management structure is based on the “three lines
模式，明確風險管理分工與管理職責。第                       of defense” model, which clarifies the roles and responsibilities of
一道防線，由各級業務單位和職能部門組                       management and business units and functional departments on
成，負責日常風險管理，參與評估本集團的                      risk management. The first line of defense consists of business
風險，制定應對措施及監控程序；第二道防                      units and functional departments at all levels, responsible for
線，由風險管理職能組成，負責風險管理的                      daily risk management, including participation in risk assessment
組織、推動和協調工作，並監察本集團的主                      of the Group, development of risk mitigation measures and
要風險及潛在風險；第三道防線，由本集團                      monitoring procedures; the second line of defense consists of risk
的內審職能組成，負責風險管理的監督和                       management functions, responsible for organizing, promoting and
檢查，開展內部風險核證，評估轄下風險管                      coordinating the risk management, and monitoring the Group’s
理及內部監控系統的成效。                             major and potential risks; the third line of defense consists of the
                                         Group’s internal audit function, responsible for risk management
                                         supervision and inspection, internal assurance activities,
                                         assessment of internal risk management and monitoring.

企業社會責任
Corporate Social Responsibility

作為中國領先的啤酒企業，本集團正在努力                      As a leading beer enterprise in China, the Group has not only been
不懈爭取優秀財務表現外，亦積極履行企業                      diligently and relentlessly pursuing excellent financial performance,

                                                                                                             To Our Shareholders

董事身份，在其獲判得直或無罪的任何民                       director shall be indemnified out of the funds of the Company
事或刑事訴訟中應訊所產生的一切責任，                       against all liability incurred by him as such director in defending any
可從本公司財政中獲得撥資賠償。                          proceedings, whether civil or criminal, in which judgement is given

 為營業額減少。而一般行銷活動所產生的相關成本則
 被分類為銷售及分銷費用中的促銷及推廣費用。                                      •   通過與分銷商簽訂的合同和發出的發票進行比
 If the promotion activities are related to future sales        較來測試本年度費用的確認和分類。
 volume, the related costs are treated as a reduction       •   tested the recognition and classification of
 of revenue. The costs related to general marketing             expenses during the year by comparing them
 activities are classified as promotion and marketing           with signed contracts with distributors and
 expenses in selling and distribution expenses.                 issued invoices.

 確定和計算期末的預提費用以及相應營業額減少或銷
 售及分銷費用的分類需要應用與分類、計量和確認有
 關的各種會計原則，這是涉及重大的管理層估計和判
 斷。
 Determining and calculating the accruals as at period
 ends and the respective classification as either
 a reduction of revenue or selling and distribution
 expenses requires application of various accounting
 principles relating to classification, measurement and
 recognition, which involved significant management
 estimates and judgements.

 由於涉及的分銷商數量眾多，及截至二零一八年十二
 月三十一日止年度的大額預提促銷及推廣費用，以及
 確認和分類待確定金額所涉及的重大判斷，我們認為
 這是一個關鍵的審計事項。
 Because of the large number of distributors involved
 and the significance of the accruals for promotion and
 marketing expenses for the year ended 31 December
 2018, together with the significant judgements
 involved in determining the amounts to be recognised
 and classified, we had identified this matter as a key
 audit matter.

其他信息                                     OTHER INFORMATION
貴公司董事須對其他信息負責。其他信息                       The directors of the Company are responsible for the other
包括年報內的所有信息，但不包括綜合財                       information. The other information comprises all of the information
務報表及我們的核數師報告。                            included in the annual report other than the consolidated financial
                                         statements and our auditor’s report thereon.

我們對綜合財務報表的意見並不涵蓋其他                       Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not
信息，我們亦不對該等其他信息發表任何                       cover the other information and we do not express any form of
形式的鑒證結論。                                 assurance conclusion thereon.

結合我們對綜合財務報表的審計，我們的                       In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial
責任是閱讀其他信息，在此過程中，考慮其                      statements, our responsibility is to read the other information and,
他信息是否與綜合財務報表或我們在審計                       in doing so, consider whether the other information is materially
過程中所了解的情況存在重大抵觸或者似                       inconsistent with the consolidated financial statements or our
乎存在重大錯誤陳述的情況。                            knowledge obtained in the audit or otherwise appears to be
                                         materially misstated.

基於我們已執行的工作，如果我們認為其                       If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is
他信息存在重大錯誤陳述，我們需要報告                       a material misstatement of this other information, we are required
該事實。在這方面，我們沒有任何報告。                       to report that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.

甲    綜合入賬                                A     CONSOLIDATION
     本集團之綜合財務報告包括本公司及                          The consolidated financial statements of the Group include
     其全部直接及間接附屬公司之財務報                          the financial statements of the Company and of all its direct
     告。於年內收購或出售之附屬公司之                          and indirect subsidiaries. The results of subsidiaries acquired
     業績乃由其實際收購日期起計或截至                          or disposed of during the year are included from the effective
     出售生效日期止（視情況而定）納入計                         date of acquisition or up to the effective date of disposal, as
     算。所有集團內部之重大交易及結餘                          appropriate. All material intra-group transactions and balances
     已於綜合入賬時對銷。                                have been eliminated on consolidation.

     於二零一零年一月一日之前，增加現                          Prior to 1 January 2010, increases in interests in existing
     有附屬公司權益與收購附屬公司的處                          subsidiaries were treated in the same manner as the
     理方法一致、即商譽或優惠承購增益                          acquisition of subsidiaries, with goodwill or a bargain
     在適當情況下被確認。就減少附屬公                          purchase gain being recognised where appropriate. For
     司權益 , 不管出售附屬公司是否導致本                       decreases in interests in subsidiaries, regardless of whether
     集團失去了該附屬公司之控制，已收                          the disposals would result in the Group losing control over
     代價與出售所佔淨資產的賬面值間之                          the subsidiaries, the difference between the consideration
     差額在綜合損益表中確認。                              received and the carrying amount of the share of net assets
                                               disposed of was recognised in the consolidated profit and
                                               loss account.

     從二零一零年一月一日起 , 本集團不涉                       From 1 January 2010 onward, changes in the Group’s
     及失去附屬公司控制權之權益變動，                          ownership interests in existing subsidiaries that do not
     均列作股權交易，不會影響商譽或損                          involve a loss of control are accounted for as equity
     益。當附屬公司的控制權因一個交易、                         transactions, with no impact on goodwill or profit or loss.
     事件或其他情況而喪失，本集團終止                          When control of subsidiary is lost as a result of a transaction,
     確認所有資產、負債和非控制股東權                          event or other circumstance, the Group derecognises all
     益的賬面金額。任何對前附屬公司之                          assets, liabilities and non-controlling interests at their carrying

乙    附屬公司                                B     SUBSIDIARIES
     附屬公司為本集團擁有控制權之實體                          A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity) over
    （包括架構實體）。當本集團承受或享                          which the Group has control. The Group controls an entity
     有參與實體所得之可變回報，且有能                          when the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable
     力透過其對實體之權利影響該等回報                          returns from its involvement with the entity and has the
     時，則本集團控制該實體。附屬公司於                         ability to affect those returns through its power over the
     控制權轉讓予本集團當日綜合入賬，                          entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which
     並於控制權終止當日終止入賬。附屬                          control is transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated
     公司之投資乃按成本值減去減值虧損                          from the date that control ceases. In the Company’s balance
     後於本公司資產負債表列賬。本公司                          sheet, investments in subsidiaries are carried at cost less any

      舊。付予出租人之款項包括本金及利                         accordance with the Group’s depreciation policy. Payments
      息，而利息則在損益表中扣除。                           to the lessor are treated as consisting of capital and interest

       額。可收回金額為資產之公平值減出                   The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value

     各結算日，以外幣為單位之貨幣項目                          at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the
     按結算日之匯率重新換算。以外幣為                          transactions. At each balance sheet date, monetary items
     單位按公允價值列賬之非貨幣項目按                          denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the
     釐定公允價值當日之匯率重新換算。                          rates prevailing on the balance sheet date. Non-monetary
     按原始成本計量而以外幣為單位之非                          items carried at fair value that are denominated in foreign

     因結算貨幣項目及換算貨幣項目而產                          Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary

     匯兌差額在綜合財務報告中確認為其                          in a foreign operation, in which case, such exchange

     年底時調整匯價，以反映外幣匯率的                            includes only outstanding foreign currency denominated
     5% 變動。若人民幣兌港元及美元貶值                          monetary items and adjusts their translation at year end for
     5%，對溢利或虧損將產生相等但相反                           a 5% change in foreign currency rates. For a 5% weakening
     的影響。                                        of Renminbi against Hong Kong Dollars and US Dollars, there
                                                 would be an equal and opposite impact on the profit or loss.

五 . 資本風險管理                                 5.    CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
     本集團實行資本管理的目的，是要確                            The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in
     保集團內實體能持續經營，同時通過                            the Group will be able to continue as a going concern
     債務與權益結餘的優化，提高利益群                            while maximising the return to stakeholders through the
     體的回報。本集團整體策略與前一年                            optimisation of the debt and equity balances. The Group’s
     度並無不同。                                      overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

     本集團的資本結構包括債務（包括附                            The capital structure of the Group consists of debt, which
     註二十五所披露的借貸）、現金及現                            includes the borrowings disclosed in Note 25, cash and cash
     金等價物、本公司權益持有人應佔權                            equivalents and equity attributable to equity holders of the
     益（包括綜合權益變動表所披露的已                            Company, comprising issued capital, reserves and retained
     發行股本、儲備及保留溢利）。                              profits as disclosed in the consolidated statement of changes
                                                 in equity.

     本集團管理層定期審議資本結構。本                            The management of the Group reviews the capital structure
     集團考慮資本成本及各類資本的相關                            regularly. The Group considers the cost of capital and the
     風險，並通過支付股息、發行新股、                            risks associated with each class of capital, and will balance
     以至作出銀行借貸或贖回現有債務等                            its overall capital structure through the payment of dividends,
     方式，對整體資本結構作出平衡。                             new share issues as well as the raise of bank borrowings or
                                                 the redemption of existing debt.

     本集團設有多項供香港全體僱員參與                           The Group operates various defined contribution retirement
     之定額供款退休計劃。該等計劃之資                           schemes which are available to all Hong Kong employees.
     產與本集團資產分開管理，並由獨立                           The assets of the schemes are held separately from those
     管理之基金持有。供款額乃根據僱員                           of the Group in an independently administered fund. The
     基本薪金之特定百份比計算，而離職                           amount of contributions is based on a specified percentage of

     員工無權享有之任何沒收供款則用以                           the basic salary of employees and any forfeited contributions
     減低本集團之供款。                                  in respect of unvested benefits of staff leavers are used to

                                                                                                                  About the Group
                                                reduce the Group’s contributions.

二十三. 貿易及其他應收款項（續）                         23. TRADE AND OTHER RECEIVABLES (Continued)
     貿易應收款項之減值使用的預期虧損                           Impairment on trade receivables is using the expected loss
     率乃基於對每個賬齡類別的客戶之信                           rates, which are based on credit assessments on each aging
     貸評估，並按影響客戶結清貿易應收                           category of customers and adjusted for forward-looking
     款項能力之前瞻性資料作出調整。                            information affecting the ability of the customers to settle the
                                                trade receivables.

     其他應收款項之減值乃按 12 個月預期                        Impairment on other receivables is measured as either
     信貸虧損或全期預期信貸虧損計量，                           12-month expected credit losses or lifetime expected credit
     視乎自初步確認起是否出現重大信貸                           loss, depending on whether there has been a significant
     風險增加。                                      increase in credit risk since the initial recognition.

     根據管理層進行之評估，本集團於結                           Based on the assessments performed by management, the
     算日的貿易及其他應收款項公允價值                           fair value of the Group’s trade and other receivables at balance
     與其賬面值相若。                                   sheet date was approximate to the corresponding carrying
                                                amount.

設計概念
Design Concept
華潤啤酒（控股）有限公司，以質量為核心推動一系列的重大戰略舉措。其中品牌重塑
方面，二零一八年推出高端產品「匠心營造」啤酒，配合中國飲食文化，以高品質啤酒搭
配優質餐桌風味美食，相得益彰。

「匠心營造」啤酒麥香濃郁，泡沫豐富細膩，入口輕盈甘冽，口感醇厚，風味獨特，久久
 回味。其品質並不限於味覺，這次年報設計亦希望從視覺上突出它的品質－「美」，包括
 其具備文化底蘊和美學寓意的包裝設計，啤酒倒進杯內所顯示的動態，以及啤酒的金光
 亮澤，整個設計充分反映本集團對追求極致質量的堅持。

China Resources Beer (Holdings) Company Limited promotes a series of major
strategic initiatives with quality at its core. In terms of rebranding initiative, the
Company launched the high-end product “Craftsmanship” in 2018 to match with
the Chinese dining and drink culture. “Craftsmanship” is the perfect high-quality
beer to pair with the best flavored food, bringing a new level of satisfaction to the
taste buds.

“Craftsmanship” has unique, creamy, rich malty flavor, bubbly and gentle
conditioning, and it is a refreshing bitter that leaves a long-lasting aftertaste. Its
quality is not limited to the taste. The design of this Annual Report also hopes to
highlight to the readers its exquisite quality in visual sense, including its beautifully
designed packaging influenced by Chinese culture, the dynamic motion of the beer
when it is poured into a glass, and its golden bright color. The whole design of the
Annual Report fully reflects the Group’s commitment to the pursuit of quality.
